I end up snapping at 'em. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel restless or on edge when you're not working on a project or task?\n\nPatient: I guess so. I just... I can't really sit still. If I sit in my chair for more than a couple minutes, I just... I have to get up. I'll start sorting boxes, or I'll go out and measure the garage again. Or I'll start loading up tools. Sometimes I just head back over to the old place. It's like... I don't know, my body just feels like it's got a motor in it right now. I just have to keep moving.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty or worthless when you're not productive?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I don't feel guilty. I just like to work. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or down most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: No. I haven't been sad. I just... I feel fine.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to focus on one thing for a long time?\n\nPatient: I suppose. Just... at the shop or at home, if I hear a clank, or a truck goes by outside... it pulls me off what I'm doing. Or I'll see a tool out of place and go over to that. Pretty soon I've got... I don't know, five things started. None of 'em finished. It just happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel extremely happy or euphoric for no particular reason?\n\nPatient: Euphoric... I don't know about that word. I just... I feel good. Joking around, feeling like I'm ten feet tall sometimes. But... well, I just need people to either get on board with my plans or get out of my way. If they don't... I don't know. My wife or my boy will ask some little question and I just... I end up snapping at 'em. I don't mean to, it's just... they're slowing me down.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually happy or on top of the world for most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: I thought I just answered that. I'm not... on top of the world or anything like that. I just... I feel fine. Like I said. Nothing unusual about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're in an unusually good mood most of the time?\n\nPatient: I... look, we just went over this. I feel fine. Just fine. I don't know what else you want me to say.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually confident or capable of handling more than usual lately?\n\nPatient: Look... I feel like I'm just repeating myself here. I told you already. Sometimes I feel ten feet tall. I've got plans, and I can handle 'em just fine. I just need everybody to either get on board or get out of my way. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually happy or irritable most days for the past few weeks?\n\nPatient: I don't know why you keep using those words. I'm not irritable, I just... it's been about a month. Ever since we were forced to start leaving the old house. That's all it is.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you're ten feet tall most of the time over the past month?\n\nPatient: I don't know about most of the time. I don't sit around keeping track of every hour of the day. Just... sometimes, like I said.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I don't sit around keeping track of every hour of the day. Sometimes I just head back over to the old place. It's like... I don't know, my body just feels like it's got a motor in it right now. I just have to keep moving.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty or worthless when you're not productive?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I don't feel guilty. I just like to work. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or down most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: No. I haven't been sad. I just... I feel fine.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to focus on one thing for a long time?\n\nPatient: I suppose. Just... at the shop or at home, if I hear a clank, or a truck goes by outside... it pulls me off what I'm doing. Or I'll see a tool out of place and go over to that. Pretty soon I've got... I don't know, five things started. None of 'em finished. It just happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel extremely happy or euphoric for no particular reason?\n\nPatient: Euphoric... I don't know about that word. I just... I feel good. Joking around, feeling like I'm ten feet tall sometimes. But... well, I just need people to either get on board with my plans or get out of my way. If they don't... I don't know. My wife or my boy will ask some little question and I just... I end up snapping at 'em. I don't mean to, it's just... they're slowing me down.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually happy or on top of the world for most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: I thought I just answered that. I'm not... on top of the world or anything like that. I just... I feel fine. Like I said. Nothing unusual about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're in an unusually good mood most of the time?\n\nPatient: I... look, we just went over this. I feel fine. Just fine. I don't know what else you want me to say.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually confident or capable of handling more than usual lately?\n\nPatient: Look... I feel like I'm just repeating myself here. I told you already. Sometimes I feel ten feet tall. I've got plans, and I can handle 'em just fine. I just need everybody to either get on board or get out of my way. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually happy or irritable most days for the past few weeks?\n\nPatient: I don't know why you keep using those words. I'm not irritable, I just... it's been about a month. Ever since we were forced to start leaving the old house. That's all it is.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you're ten feet tall most of the time over the past month?\n\nPatient: I don't know about most of the time. I don't sit around keeping track of every hour of the day. Just... sometimes, like I said.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're ten feet tall every day for the past month?\n\nPatient: I just said I don't keep track. No. Probably not every single day. I just... I wake up and handle my business. That's all I know.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're ten feet tall for most of each day over the past week?\n\nPatient: I don't... I really don't think so. I'm just trying to get through the week, alright? I don't know why you're so focused on this ten feet tall thing. I'm just regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt unusually happy or on top of the world at any point over the past week?\n\nPatient: I don't know about 'on top of the world.' I just... I have plans. Things to do. And it just feels like everybody needs to either get on board with them or... or just get out of my way. \n\nI guess sometimes I'll go from joking around, feeling good... maybe that's your ten feet tall thing... to just snapping at my wife or my boy over some little question. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like you're ten feet tall or exceptionally good for most of the day, every day, for the past two weeks?\n\nPatient: No. I already said I don't feel like that. I'm just regular. Two weeks, one week, it doesn't matter. I don't know why we keep going over this.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt unusually good or happy for most of the day today?\n\nPatient: No. Like I've been saying... I just feel regular. Today is just another day. I don't know what else to tell you.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like your good mood has lasted all day without changing for the past few days?\n\nPatient: I... no. I haven't been in a good mood all day. I already said I'm just regular. I don't... I don't really pay attention to if my mood changes all day long. I don't know what you're looking for here.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt like your mood has been better than normal for most of the time lately?\n\nPatient: I don't know about better. I just... I guess I feel like everybody needs to either get on board with my plans or just get out of my way. \n\nI mean...
